An accordion is a container for multiple accordion items. Import it from the Creative Univ...

<Description>
  An accordion is a container for multiple accordion items. Import it from the Creative Universe
  Spectrum wrapper to receive Spectrum styling, color scheme support, and Adobe Clean typography
  automatically.
</Description>

## Basic usage

<Source
  language="tsx"
  code={`import {
  Accordion,
  AccordionItem,
  AccordionItemPanel,
  AccordionItemTitle,
} from "@/components/spectrum/Accordion";

<Accordion>
  <AccordionItem id="personal">
    <AccordionItemTitle>Personal Information</AccordionItemTitle>
    <AccordionItemPanel>Personal information form here.</AccordionItemPanel>
  </AccordionItem>
  <AccordionItem id="billing">
    <AccordionItemTitle>Billing Address</AccordionItemTitle>
    <AccordionItemPanel>Billing address form here.</AccordionItemPanel>
  </AccordionItem>
</Accordion>`}
/>

<Primary />

## Expanding

Use `defaultExpandedKeys` for the initial uncontrolled state. Use `expandedKeys` with `onExpandedChange` to control which items are open. The expanded keys correspond to each `AccordionItem` `id`.

<Source
  language="tsx"
  code={`import { useState } from "react";
import {
  Accordion,
  AccordionItem,
  AccordionItemPanel,
  AccordionItemTitle,
  type Key,
} from "@/components/spectrum/Accordion";

function Example() {
const [expandedKeys, setExpandedKeys] = useState(new Set<Key>(["settings"]));

return (
<Accordion expandedKeys={expandedKeys} onExpandedChange={setExpandedKeys}>
<AccordionItem id="settings">
<AccordionItemTitle>Settings</AccordionItemTitle>
<AccordionItemPanel>Application settings content.</AccordionItemPanel>
</AccordionItem>
</Accordion>
);
}`}
/>

Set `allowsMultipleExpanded` when several items may stay open at the same time.

## Content

Use `AccordionItemHeader` to add elements next to an item title, such as icons or action buttons.

<Source
  language="tsx"
  code={`import { ActionButton } from "@react-spectrum/s2/ActionButton";
import {
  Accordion,
  AccordionItem,
  AccordionItemHeader,
  AccordionItemPanel,
  AccordionItemTitle,
} from "@/components/spectrum/Accordion";

<Accordion>
  <AccordionItem id="project">
    <AccordionItemHeader>
      <AccordionItemTitle>Project Settings</AccordionItemTitle>
      <ActionButton aria-label="Edit project settings">Edit</ActionButton>
    </AccordionItemHeader>
    <AccordionItemPanel>
      Configure your project settings including name, description, and permissions.
    </AccordionItemPanel>
  </AccordionItem>
</Accordion>`}
/>

<Stories title="Examples" include={["Controlled", "MultipleExpanded", "WithHeaderActions"]} />

## API

```tsx
<Accordion>
  <AccordionItem>
    <AccordionItemHeader>
      <AccordionItemTitle />
    </AccordionItemHeader>
    <AccordionItemPanel />
  </AccordionItem>
</Accordion>
```

### Accordion

| Name                                | Type                             | Default   | Description                                                        |
| ----------------------------------- | -------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `allowsMultipleExpanded`            | `boolean`                        | —         | Whether multiple accordion items can be expanded at the same time. |
| `defaultExpandedKeys`               | `Iterable<Key>`                  | —         | The initial expanded keys in the uncontrolled state.               |
| `expandedKeys`                      | `Iterable<Key>`                  | —         | The currently expanded keys in the controlled state.               |
| `onExpandedChange`                  | `(keys: Set<Key>) => void`       | —         | Called when accordion items are expanded or collapsed.             |
| `density`                           | `compact \| regular \| spacious` | `regular` | The amount of space between accordion items.                       |
| `size`                              | `S \| M \| L \| XL`              | `M`       | The size of the accordion.                                         |
| `isDisabled`                        | `boolean`                        | `false`   | Whether all accordion items are disabled.                          |
| `isQuiet`                           | `boolean`                        | `false`   | Whether the accordion uses a quiet style.                          |
| `styles`                            | `StylesPropWithHeight`           | —         | Spectrum-defined styles returned by the style macro.               |
| `UNSAFE_className` / `UNSAFE_style` | `string` / `CSSProperties`       | —         | Last-resort custom CSS escape hatches.                             |

### AccordionItem

| Name                         | Type                            | Default   | Description                                       |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------------------- |
| `id`                         | `Key`                           | —         | Identifier that matches `expandedKeys`.           |
| `defaultExpanded`            | `boolean`                       | `false`   | Whether the item is initially expanded.           |
| `isExpanded`                 | `boolean`                       | —         | Controlled expanded state for an individual item. |
| `onExpandedChange`           | `(isExpanded: boolean) => void` | —         | Called when the item changes expanded state.      |
| `isDisabled`                 | `boolean`                       | `false`   | Whether the item is disabled.                     |
| `density`, `size`, `isQuiet` | —                               | Inherited | Item-level display options.                       |

### AccordionItemHeader and AccordionItemTitle

`AccordionItemHeader` accepts the header content. `AccordionItemTitle` renders the item heading and accepts `level`, which defaults to `3`. Both also support `id`, `UNSAFE_className`, and `UNSAFE_style`.

### AccordionItemPanel

`AccordionItemPanel` contains the expandable content. It supports standard accessibility labels and `role`, which defaults to `group`; use `region` when the panel needs a landmark role.

## Controls

Use the Storybook Controls panel to QA `allowsMultipleExpanded`, `density`, `isDisabled`, `isQuiet`, and `size`.

<Controls />

./src/components/spectrum/ActionBar/ActionBar.docs.mdx
Action bars are used for single and bulk selection patterns when a user needs to perform a...
import { Meta, Title, Description, Primary, Controls, Source } from "@storybook/addon-docs/blocks";
import * as ActionBarStories from "./ActionBar.stories";

<Meta of={ActionBarStories} />
<Title />
<Description>
Action bars are used for single and bulk selection patterns when a user needs to perform actions on one or more items at the same time. This wrapper automatically applies React Spectrum S2 styles, typography, color schemes, and accessibility behavior.
</Description>

## QA color scheme

Use the **Spectrum Light** or **Spectrum Dark** toolbar control in Storybook to inspect both color schemes.

## Basic usage

Pass `selectedItemCount` and place one or more `ActionButton` components inside the bar. The bar is hidden when the count is `0`.

<Source language="tsx" code={`import { ActionBar, ActionButton } from "@/components/spectrum/ActionBar";

<ActionBar selectedItemCount={2}>
  <ActionButton aria-label="Edit">Edit</ActionButton>
  <ActionButton aria-label="Copy">Copy</ActionButton>
  <ActionButton aria-label="Delete">Delete</ActionButton>
</ActionBar>`} />
<Primary />

## TableView, ListView, and TreeView

ActionBar is intended to be rendered by collection components through `renderActionBar`. Pass the selected collection size to `selectedItemCount` and keep the actions inside the bar. The same pattern works with `ListView` and `TreeView`.

<Source language="tsx" code={`<TableView
  aria-label="Table with action bar"
  selectionMode="multiple"
  renderActionBar={(selectedKeys) => (
    <ActionBar selectedItemCount={selectedKeys.size}>
      <ActionButton aria-label="Edit">Edit</ActionButton>
      <ActionButton aria-label="Copy">Copy</ActionButton>
      <ActionButton aria-label="Delete">Delete</ActionButton>
    </ActionBar>
  )}
>`} />

## Clearing selection

Use `onClearSelection` to connect the built-in clear-selection button to the state owned by the collection.

<Source language="tsx" code={`<ActionBar
  selectedItemCount={selectedKeys.size}
  onClearSelection={() => setSelectedKeys(new Set())}
>
  <ActionButton aria-label="Archive">Archive</ActionButton>
</ActionBar>`} />

## All items and emphasis

Use `selectedItemCount="all"` when every item is selected. Set `isEmphasized` to `true` when the actions need stronger visual emphasis.

<Source language="tsx" code={`<ActionBar selectedItemCount="all" isEmphasized>
  <ActionButton aria-label="Archive">Archive</ActionButton>
</ActionBar>`} />

## API

<table>
  <thead>
    <tr><th>Name</th><th>Type</th><th>Default</th><th>Description</th></tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
    <tr><td><code>children</code></td><td><code>ReactNode</code></td><td>-</td><td>A list of ActionButtons to display.</td></tr>
    <tr><td><code>id</code></td><td><code>string | undefined</code></td><td>-</td><td>The element's unique identifier.</td></tr>
    <tr><td><code>isEmphasized</code></td><td><code>boolean | undefined</code></td><td>-</td><td>Whether the ActionBar uses an emphasized style.</td></tr>
    <tr><td><code>onClearSelection</code></td><td><code>(() =&gt; void) | undefined</code></td><td>-</td><td>Handler called when the clear-selection button is pressed.</td></tr>
    <tr><td><code>scrollRef</code></td><td><code>RefObject&lt;HTMLElement | null&gt; | undefined</code></td><td>-</td><td>Ref to the scrollable element above which the bar appears.</td></tr>
    <tr><td><code>selectedItemCount</code></td><td><code>number | "all" | undefined</code></td><td>-</td><td>Number of selected items; hidden when <code>0</code>.</td></tr>
    <tr><td><code>slot</code></td><td><code>string | null | undefined</code></td><td>-</td><td>Slot name for receiving parent props.</td></tr>
    <tr><td><code>styles</code></td><td><code>StylesProp | undefined</code></td><td>-</td><td>Spectrum styles returned by the <code>style()</code> macro.</td></tr>
    <tr><td><code>UNSAFE_className</code></td><td><code>UnsafeClassName | undefined</code></td><td>-</td><td>Custom class name, intended only as a last resort.</td></tr>
    <tr><td><code>UNSAFE_style</code></td><td><code>CSSProperties | undefined</code></td><td>-</td><td>Inline style, intended only as a last resort.</td></tr>
  </tbody>
</table>

`ActionButton` is re-exported from this wrapper for convenience. Its props include `aria-label`, `isDisabled`, `isQuiet`, `isPending`, `onPress`, and other React Spectrum S2 action behavior.

ActionButtons allow users to perform an action. They are used for similar, task-based opti...
import { Meta, Title, Description, Primary, Controls, Stories, Source } from "@storybook/addon-docs/blocks";
import * as ActionButtonStories from "./ActionButton.stories";

<Meta of={ActionButtonStories} />
<Title />
<Description>
ActionButtons allow users to perform an action. They are used for similar, task-based options within a workflow and are ideal for interfaces where buttons are not meant to draw a lot of attention. This wrapper automatically applies React Spectrum S2 styling, typography, color schemes, and accessibility behavior.
</Description>

## QA color scheme

Use the **Spectrum Light** or **Spectrum Dark** toolbar control in Storybook to inspect every state in both color schemes.

## Basic usage

<Source language="tsx" code={`import { ActionButton } from "@/components/spectrum/ActionButton";

<ActionButton>Edit</ActionButton>`} />
<Primary />

ActionButtons can contain an icon, text, avatar, or other Spectrum content. Provide `aria-label` when the button has no visible text label.

<Source language="tsx" code={`import { ActionButton } from "@/components/spectrum/ActionButton";
import Cut from "@react-spectrum/s2/icons/Cut";

<ActionButton aria-label="Cut"><Cut />Cut</ActionButton>`} />

## Events

Use `onPress` to handle mouse, keyboard, and touch interactions. `onPressStart`, `onPressEnd`, and `onPressChange` are also emitted during the interaction lifecycle.

<Source language="tsx" code={`import { useState } from "react";
import { ActionButton } from "@/components/spectrum/ActionButton";

function Example() {
  const [count, setCount] = useState(0);

  return (
    <ActionButton onPress={() => setCount((value) => value + 1)}>
      {count} Edits
    </ActionButton>
  );
}`} />

## Pending

Set `isPending` to display an indeterminate spinner. Pending buttons remain focusable but do not accept press or hover interactions while pending.

<Source language="tsx" code={`import { useState } from "react";
import { ActionButton } from "@/components/spectrum/ActionButton";

function PendingButton() {
  const [isPending, setPending] = useState(false);

  return (
    <ActionButton
      isPending={isPending}
      onPress={() => {
        setPending(true);
        setTimeout(() => setPending(false), 5000);
      }}>
      Save
    </ActionButton>
  );
}`} />

## Appearance and state

- `isQuiet` applies the quiet ActionButton style.
- `isDisabled` disables the button.
- `size` supports `XS`, `S`, `M`, `L`, and `XL`.
- `staticColor` supports `auto`, `black`, and `white` when the button appears over a colored background.
- Use `type="submit"` or `type="reset"` when the button participates in a form.

<Stories title="Examples" include={["WithIcon", "Quiet", "Disabled", "Pending", "InteractivePending"]} />

"use client";

import { forwardRef, type ComponentRef } from "react";
import {
  ActionButtonGroup as SpectrumActionButtonGroup,
  ActionButton,
  Text,
  type ActionButtonGroupProps,
} from "@react-spectrum/s2/ActionButtonGroup";

export { ActionButton, Text };
export type { ActionButtonGroupProps };

An ActionButtonGroup is a grouping of related ActionButtons. This wrapper uses React Spect...
import { Meta, Title, Description, Primary, Controls, Stories, Source } from "@storybook/addon-docs/blocks";
import * as ActionButtonGroupStories from "./ActionButtonGroup.stories";

<Meta of={ActionButtonGroupStories} />
<Title />
<Description>
An ActionButtonGroup is a grouping of related ActionButtons. This wrapper uses React Spectrum S2 and automatically applies Spectrum styling, typography, color schemes, and accessibility behavior.
</Description>

## QA color scheme

Use the **Spectrum Light** or **Spectrum Dark** toolbar control in Storybook to inspect the group in both color schemes.

## Basic usage

Place related ActionButtons inside the group. Use `Text` alongside an icon to provide visible labels.

<Source language="tsx" code={`import { ActionButtonGroup, ActionButton, Text } from "@/components/spectrum/ActionButtonGroup";
import Cut from "@react-spectrum/s2/icons/Cut";
import Copy from "@react-spectrum/s2/icons/Copy";
import Paste from "@react-spectrum/s2/icons/Paste";

<ActionButtonGroup>
  <ActionButton aria-label="Cut"><Cut /><Text>Cut</Text></ActionButton>
  <ActionButton aria-label="Copy"><Copy /><Text>Copy</Text></ActionButton>
  <ActionButton aria-label="Paste"><Paste /><Text>Paste</Text></ActionButton>
</ActionButtonGroup>`} />
<Primary />

## Orientation and density

Set `orientation="vertical"` to stack buttons. Use `density="compact"` for tighter spacing, or keep the default `regular` density for standard spacing.

<Source language="tsx" code={`<ActionButtonGroup orientation="vertical" density="compact">
  <ActionButton aria-label="Cut">Cut</ActionButton>
  <ActionButton aria-label="Copy">Copy</ActionButton>
</ActionButtonGroup>`} />

## Justified and quiet groups

Set `isJustified` to divide the available width equally between buttons. Set `isQuiet` when the group should use the quiet ActionButton style. Use `staticColor="black"` or `staticColor="white"` when the group appears over a colored background.

## Disabled state

Set `isDisabled` on the group to disable all ActionButtons in the group consistently.

<Stories title="Examples" include={["Vertical", "Compact", "Justified", "Disabled", "Quiet"]} />

./src/components/spectrum/ActionMenu/ActionMenu.docs.mdx
# ActionMenu ActionMenu combines an ActionButton with a Menu for simple “more actions” use...
import {Canvas, Controls, Meta} from '@storybook/addon-docs/blocks';
import * as ActionMenuStories from './ActionMenu.stories';

<Meta of={ActionMenuStories} />

# ActionMenu

ActionMenu combines an ActionButton with a Menu for simple “more actions” use cases. It provides Spectrum styling, keyboard navigation, focus management, and accessible menu semantics.

## Basic usage

```tsx
import {ActionMenu, Keyboard, MenuItem, Text} from '@/components/spectrum/ActionMenu';

<ActionMenu aria-label="More actions">
  <MenuItem textValue="Copy" onAction={() => copySelection()}>
    <Text slot="label">Copy</Text>
    <Text slot="description">Copy the selected text</Text>
    <Keyboard>⌘C</Keyboard>
  </MenuItem>
</ActionMenu>
```

<Canvas of={ActionMenuStories.Default} />

## Variants and states

Use `isQuiet` for a low-emphasis trigger, `isDisabled` to disable the entire menu, and `disabledKeys` to disable specific menu items. `size` controls the trigger while `menuSize` controls the menu.

<Canvas of={ActionMenuStories.Quiet} />
<Canvas of={ActionMenuStories.DisabledItems} />

## Controlled open state

Use `isOpen` with `onOpenChange` when the open state must be controlled by the parent.

<Canvas of={ActionMenuStories.Controlled} />

## Placement and accessibility

Use `direction`, `align`, and `shouldFlip` to control placement. The menu flips automatically by default when space is limited. Provide an accessible name with `aria-label` or `aria-labelledby`, and use `textValue` for items whose visible content is not plain text.

## API

| Name | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `align` | `"end" \| "start"` | `start` | Alignment of the menu relative to the trigger. |
| `children` | `ReactNode \| (item: T) => ReactNode` | — | The contents of the collection. |
| `defaultOpen` | `boolean` | — | Whether the overlay is open by default. |
| `direction` | `"bottom" \| "end" \| "left" \| "right" \| "start" \| "top"` | `bottom` | Where the menu opens relative to its trigger. |
| `disabledKeys` | `Iterable<Key>` | — | Items that cannot be selected or focused. |
| `isDisabled` | `boolean` | — | Whether the button is disabled. |
| `isOpen` | `boolean` | — | Whether the overlay is controlled as open. |
| `isQuiet` | `boolean` | — | Whether the trigger uses a quiet style. |
| `items` | `Iterable<T>` | — | Item objects in the collection. |
| `menuSize` | `"L" \| "M" \| "S" \| "XL"` | `M` | The size of the Menu. |
| `onAction` | `(key: Key, value: T) => void` | — | Called when an item is selected. |
| `onOpenChange` | `(isOpen: boolean) => void` | — | Called when the overlay open state changes. |
| `shouldCloseOnSelect` | `boolean` | — | Whether the menu closes when an item is selected. |
| `shouldFlip` | `boolean` | `true` | Whether the menu automatically flips when space is limited. |
| `size` | `"L" \| "M" \| "S" \| "XL" \| "XS"` | `M` | Size of the ActionButton trigger. |
| `styles` | `StylesProp` | — | Spectrum-defined styles. |
| `UNSAFE_className` | `UnsafeClassName` | — | Custom class name, intended only as a last resort. |
| `UNSAFE_style` | `CSSProperties` | — | Inline style, intended only as a last resort. |

## Controls

# Avatar An avatar is a thumbnail representation of an entity, such as a user or an organi...
import {Canvas, Controls, Meta} from "@storybook/addon-docs/blocks";

# Avatar

An avatar is a thumbnail representation of an entity, such as a user or an organization. This wrapper uses React Spectrum S2 styling and preserves its accessible image behavior.

## Basic usage

```tsx
import {Avatar} from "@/components/spectrum/Avatar";

<Avatar src="/images/user.png" alt="Alex Johnson" />
```

<Canvas of={AvatarStories.Default} />

## Sizes

Use the `size` prop to select a supported Spectrum size or provide a numeric size. The default size is `24`.

<Canvas of={AvatarStories.Small} />
<Canvas of={AvatarStories.Large} />

## Backgrounds and fallback

Set `isOverBackground` when the avatar appears over a color background. If no `src` is supplied, Spectrum renders the appropriate fallback treatment.

<Canvas of={AvatarStories.OverBackground} />
<Canvas of={AvatarStories.WithoutImage} />

## Accessibility

Always provide meaningful `alt` text when the avatar communicates identity. If the avatar is purely decorative, use an empty `alt` value. The image URL is passed through the `src` prop.

## API

| Name | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `alt` | `string` | — | Text description of the avatar. |
| `id` | `string` | — | The element's unique identifier. |
| `isOverBackground` | `boolean` | — | Whether the avatar is over a color background. |
| `size` | `16 \| 20 \| 24 \| 28 \| 32 \| 36 \| 40 \| 44 \| 48 \| 56 \| 64 \| 80 \| 96 \| 112 \| number` | `24` | The size of the avatar. |
| `slot` | `string \| null` | — | A slot name for receiving parent props. |
| `src` | `string` | — | The image URL for the avatar. |
| `styles` | `StylesPropWithoutWidth` | — | Spectrum-defined styles returned by the style macro. |
| `UNSAFE_className` | `UnsafeClassName` | — | Custom class name, intended only as a last resort. |
| `UNSAFE_style` | `CSSProperties` | — | Inline style, intended only as a last resort. |

# AvatarGroup

An avatar group is a grouping of avatars that are related to each other, such as project members or participants.

## Basic usage

```tsx
import {AvatarGroup, Avatar} from "@/components/spectrum/AvatarGroup";

<AvatarGroup label="Project members">
  <Avatar alt="Abraham Baker" src="/images/abraham-baker.png" />
  <Avatar alt="Adriana Sullivan" src="/images/adriana-sullivan.png" />
</AvatarGroup>
```

<Canvas of={AvatarGroupStories.Default} />

## Sizes

Use `size` to set the size of every avatar in the group. Supported values are `16`, `20`, `24`, `28`, `32`, `36`, and `40`.

<Canvas of={AvatarGroupStories.Small} />
<Canvas of={AvatarGroupStories.Large} />

## Accessibility

Provide a meaningful `label` or accessible labeling prop to describe the group. Each child Avatar should have meaningful `alt` text when it communicates identity.

## API

| Name | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `aria-describedby` | `string` | — | Identifies the element that describes the group. |
| `aria-details` | `string` | — | Identifies the element that provides detailed information. |
| `aria-label` | `string` | — | Defines an accessible name for the group. |
| `aria-labelledby` | `string` | — | References the element that labels the group. |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | — | Avatar children of the avatar group. |
| `id` | `string` | — | The element's unique identifier. |
| `label` | `string` | — | The label for the avatar group. |
| `size` | `16 \| 20 \| 24 \| 28 \| 32 \| 36 \| 40` | `24` | The size of the avatar group. |
| `slot` | `string \| null` | — | A slot name for receiving parent props. |
| `styles` | `StylesPropWithoutWidth` | — | Spectrum-defined styles returned by the style macro. |
| `UNSAFE_className` | `UnsafeClassName` | — | Custom class name, intended only as a last resort. |
| `UNSAFE_style` | `CSSProperties` | — | Inline style, intended only as a last resort. |

# Badge

Badges show a small amount of color-categorized metadata and are ideal for getting a user's attention.

## Basic usage

```tsx
import {Badge} from "@/components/spectrum/Badge";

<Badge variant="informative">In progress</Badge>
```

<Canvas of={BadgeStories.Default} />

## Semantic variants

Use semantic variants such as `positive`, `negative`, `notice`, or `informative` when the badge communicates meaning. Use color variants such as `blue`, `purple`, or `green` for non-semantic categorization.

<Canvas of={BadgeStories.SemanticPositive} />
<Canvas of={BadgeStories.SemanticNegative} />

## Fill styles and overflow

Use `bold`, `outline`, or `subtle` with `fillStyle` to control the visual treatment. Set `overflowMode` to `truncate` when the badge must stay on one line, or use `wrap` for longer content.

<Canvas of={BadgeStories.Outline} />
<Canvas of={BadgeStories.Subtle} />
<Canvas of={BadgeStories.Truncated} />

## Accessibility

Badge content should be concise and understandable without relying only on color. Use semantic variants when applicable and provide `aria-label` when the visible content does not fully describe the badge.

## API

| Name | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `aria-describedby` | `string` | — | Identifies the element that describes the badge. |
| `aria-details` | `string` | — | Identifies detailed information about the badge. |
| `aria-label` | `string` | — | Defines an accessible name for the badge. |
| `aria-labelledby` | `string` | — | References the element that labels the badge. |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | — | The content to display in the badge. |
| `fillStyle` | `"bold" \| "outline" \| "subtle"` | `bold` | The fill treatment of the badge. |
| `id` | `string` | — | The element's unique identifier. |
| `overflowMode` | `"truncate" \| "wrap"` | `wrap` | Sets text behavior for badge contents. |
| `size` | `"S" \| "M" \| "L" \| "XL"` | `S` | The size of the badge. |
| `slot` | `string \| null` | — | A slot name for receiving parent props. |
| `styles` | `StylesProp` | — | Spectrum-defined styles returned by the style macro. |
| `UNSAFE_className` | `UnsafeClassName` | — | Custom class name, intended only as a last resort. |
| `UNSAFE_style` | `CSSProperties` | — | Inline style, intended only as a last resort. |
| `variant` | `accent \| blue \| brown \| celery \| chartreuse \| cinnamon \| cyan \| fuchsia \| gray \| green \| indigo \| informative \| magenta \| negative \| neutral \| notice \| orange \| pink \| positive \| purple \| red \| seafoam \| silver \| turquoise \| yellow` | `neutral` | Changes the background color or semantic meaning of the badge. |

# Button Buttons allow users to perform an action. This wrapper exposes the React Spectrum...
import { Meta, Canvas, Controls } from '@storybook/addon-docs/blocks';
import * as Stories from './Button.stories';

<Meta of={Stories} />

# Button

Buttons allow users to perform an action. This wrapper exposes the React Spectrum S2 Button API while applying the local Spectrum baseline automatically.

## Basic usage

Use the `Button` component with an `onPress` handler. The `onPress` event works consistently with mouse, keyboard, and touch interactions.

<Canvas of={Stories.Primary} />

## Variants and sizes

Use `variant` to communicate the action hierarchy and `size` to match the surrounding layout. `fillStyle="outline"` creates an outlined button.

<Canvas of={Stories.VariantsAndSizes} />

## Disabled and pending states

Use `isDisabled` when the action cannot currently be performed. Use `isPending` for asynchronous work; pending buttons remain focusable while their interaction is disabled and Spectrum announces the pending state.

<Canvas of={Stories.Disabled} />

<Canvas of={Stories.Pending} />

## Events

Use `onPress`, `onPressStart`, `onPressEnd`, or `onPressChange` for interaction callbacks. These callbacks are local Storybook fixtures and do not call an external service.

<Canvas of={Stories.PressEvents} />

## Accessibility and theming

Use visible text for the action whenever possible. For icon-only buttons, provide an accessible `aria-label`. Button styles, color scheme, focus behavior, and keyboard interaction are provided by React Spectrum S2. Verify the component in both Spectrum Light and Dark themes.

## API

<Controls />

The wrapper supports the full S2 Button API, including `variant`, `fillStyle`, `size`, `isDisabled`, `isPending`, `type`, `onPress`, `styles`, and accessibility properties.

Calendar menampilkan grid hari dalam satu atau beberapa bulan untuk memilih satu tanggal. ...
import { Meta, Title, Description, Primary, Controls, Stories, Source } from "@storybook/addon-docs/blocks";
import * as CalendarStories from "./Calendar.stories";

<Meta of={CalendarStories} />

<Title />

<Description>
Calendar menampilkan grid hari dalam satu atau beberapa bulan untuk memilih satu tanggal. Component ini adalah wrapper React Spectrum S2, sehingga style dan aksesibilitas Spectrum aktif otomatis saat diimpor dari `@/components/spectrum/Calendar`.
</Description>

## QA color scheme

Gunakan toolbar **Spectrum Light** atau **Spectrum Dark** di bagian atas Storybook untuk memeriksa tampilan Calendar pada kedua color scheme. Kontrol ini hanya memengaruhi story kategori Spectrum.

## Penggunaan dasar

<Source
  language="tsx"
  code={`import { Calendar } from "@/components/spectrum/Calendar";

<Calendar aria-label="Tanggal pilihan" />`}
/>

`aria-label` wajib diberikan apabila Calendar tidak memiliki label visual yang terhubung. Gunakan `defaultValue` untuk nilai awal yang tidak dikontrol.

<Primary />

## Nilai controlled

Gunakan `value` dan `onChange` ketika tanggal perlu disimpan pada state atau disinkronkan dengan form.

<Source
  language="tsx"
  code={`import { parseDate } from "@internationalized/date";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Calendar } from "@/components/spectrum/Calendar";

function Example() {
  const [date, setDate] = useState(parseDate("2025-02-03"));

  return <Calendar aria-label="Tanggal pilihan" value={date} onChange={setDate} />;
}`}
/>

<Stories title="Contoh interaktif" include={["Controlled", "TwoMonths", "Disabled"]} />

## Validasi dan ketersediaan tanggal

Batasi rentang dengan `minValue` dan `maxValue`. Gunakan `isDateUnavailable` untuk aturan tambahan seperti tanggal penuh atau akhir pekan. `isInvalid` dan `errorMessage` digunakan jika aturan validasi berasal dari aplikasi.

<Source
  language="tsx"
  code={`import { getLocalTimeZone, isWeekend, today } from "@internationalized/date";

const now = today(getLocalTimeZone());

<Calendar
  aria-label="Tanggal janji temu"
  minValue={now}
  maxValue={now.add({ months: 3 })}
  isDateUnavailable={(date) => isWeekend(date, "id-ID")}
/>`}
/>

## Tampilan dan fokus

- `visibleMonths` menampilkan beberapa bulan sekaligus.
- `firstDayOfWeek` mengatur awal minggu.
- `focusedValue` dan `onFocusChange` mengontrol bulan yang sedang terlihat.
- `pageBehavior="visible"` berpindah sebesar jumlah bulan terlihat; gunakan `"single"` untuk berpindah satu bulan.
- `isDisabled` menonaktifkan Calendar; `isReadOnly` mempertahankan nilai tetapi tidak mengizinkan perubahan.

## Internasionalisasi

Calendar memakai locale pengguna secara default. Berikan nilai dari `@internationalized/date` untuk parsing, manipulasi tanggal, zona waktu, atau sistem kalender lain. Untuk mengubah sistem kalender tampilan, gunakan `Provider` Spectrum dengan locale yang memakai Unicode calendar extension.

<Source
  language="tsx"
  code={`import { parseDate } from "@internationalized/date";
import { Provider } from "@react-spectrum/s2/Provider";
import { Calendar } from "@/components/spectrum/Calendar";

<Provider locale="id-ID-u-ca-gregory">
  <Calendar aria-label="Tanggal pilihan" defaultValue={parseDate("2025-02-03")} />
</Provider>`}
/>

## API dan Controls

Gunakan panel Controls untuk mencoba props yang dapat dikonfigurasi. Props penting lainnya yang diteruskan langsung ke Calendar Spectrum: `defaultValue`, `value`, `onChange`, `minValue`, `maxValue`, `isDateUnavailable`, `focusedValue`, `onFocusChange`, `selectionAlignment`, `errorMessage`, dan `createCalendar`.

<Controls />

## Basic usage

<Source language="tsx" code={`import {Card, CardPreview, Content, Image, Text} from "@/components/spectrum/Card";

<Card aria-label="Project Aurora">
  <CardPreview><Image alt="Project preview" src={preview} /></CardPreview>
  <Content>
    <Text slot="title">Project Aurora</Text>
    <Text slot="description">A concise project overview.</Text>
  </Content>
</Card>`} />

<Primary />

## Variants and states

Use `size`, `density`, and `variant` to adjust the card presentation. Use `isDisabled` to prevent interaction and `href` to make the card navigable. The `AssetCard`, `UserCard`, and `ProductCard` variants provide predefined layouts.

<Controls />
<Stories include={["Quiet", "Disabled", "Linked", "Asset", "User", "Product"]} />

## Accessibility and internationalization

Provide an accessible label when the card does not contain an equivalent visible label. Card content uses the Spectrum S2 locale and color scheme context supplied by the consuming application boundary.

## QA color schemes

Use the Storybook Spectrum Light and Spectrum Dark toolbar controls to verify both color schemes.

import { forwardRef, type ComponentRef } from "react";

  ComboBox as SpectrumComboBox,
  ComboBoxItem,
  ComboBoxSection,
  Header,
  Heading,
  Text,
  type ComboBoxItemProps,

    </div>
  );

./src/components/spectrum/ComboBox/ComboBox.docs.mdx
# ComboBox ComboBox allows users to choose a single option from a collapsible list when sp...
import { Meta, Canvas, Controls } from '@storybook/addon-docs/blocks';
import * as Stories from './ComboBox.stories';

<Meta of={Stories} />

# ComboBox

ComboBox allows users to choose a single option from a collapsible list when space is limited.

## Basic usage

Use `ComboBoxItem` children with stable `id` values for selectable options. The wrapper preserves the React Spectrum S2 API and automatically applies the Spectrum baseline through `.spectrum-component`.

<Canvas of={Stories.Default} />

## Content and sections

Items can contain label and description slots. Use `ComboBoxSection` with `Header`, `Heading`, and `Text` to group related options.

<Canvas of={Stories.WithDescriptions} />

<Canvas of={Stories.WithSections} />

## State

Use `defaultSelectedKey` for uncontrolled selection or `selectedKey` with `onSelectionChange` for controlled selection. `isDisabled`, `isRequired`, `isInvalid`, `loadingState`, and `onLoadMore` support common form and asynchronous states.

<Canvas of={Stories.Disabled} />

## Accessibility

Provide a visible `label` or an accessible `aria-label`. ComboBox items should contain text or decorative graphics only; interactive controls inside items break keyboard and screen reader navigation.

## API

<Controls />

See the React Spectrum S2 ComboBox API for the complete prop and collection contract.

"use client";

import { forwardRef, type ComponentRef } from "react";
import {
  Content as SpectrumContent,
  Heading as SpectrumHeading,
  InlineAlert as SpectrumInlineAlert,
  type InlineAlertProps,
} from "@react-spectrum/s2/InlineAlert";

export { SpectrumContent as Content, SpectrumHeading as Heading };
export type { InlineAlertProps };

type InlineAlertRef = ComponentRef<typeof SpectrumInlineAlert>;

export const InlineAlert = forwardRef<InlineAlertRef, InlineAlertProps>(function InlineAlert(props, ref) {
  return (
    <div className="spectrum-component">
      <SpectrumInlineAlert {...props} ref={ref} />
    </div>
  );
});

# InlineAlert `InlineAlert` displays non-modal feedback associated with content in a view,...
import { Meta, Canvas, Controls } from '@storybook/addon-docs/blocks';
import * as Stories from './InlineAlert.stories';

<Meta of={Stories} />

# InlineAlert

`InlineAlert` displays non-modal feedback associated with content in a view, such as form validation or operation status.

## Basic usage

Use `Heading` and `Content` from this wrapper to provide an accessible alert structure.

<Canvas of={Stories.Default} />

## Variants

Use semantic variants such as `negative`, `positive`, `notice`, `informative`, and `neutral` with `border`, `subtleFill`, or `boldFill` fill styles.

<Canvas of={Stories.Negative} />

<Controls />

# LinkButton This compatibility adapter combines the Spectrum Button appearance with link ...
import { Meta, Canvas } from "@storybook/addon-docs/blocks";
import * as Stories from './LinkButton.stories';

<Meta of={Stories} />

# LinkButton

This compatibility adapter combines the Spectrum Button appearance with link navigation. The installed S2 package does not currently export a `LinkButton` subpath, so navigation is handled by the adapter while visual behavior comes from the official S2 Button.

<Canvas of={Stories.Basic} />

Use `href`, `target`, `rel`, `isDisabled`, and the regular S2 button props. Browser navigation is not triggered while disabled.

./src/components/spectrum/SideNav/SideNav.docs.mdx
# SideNav A SideNav provides users with a way to navigate a nested hierarchical set of lin...
import {Canvas, Controls, Meta} from "@storybook/addon-docs/blocks";
import * as SideNavStories from "./SideNav.stories";

<Meta of={SideNavStories} />

# SideNav

A SideNav provides users with a way to navigate a nested hierarchical set of links. It supports static and dynamic collections, expansion, routing, sections, disabled items, and accessible navigation semantics.

## Basic usage

```tsx
import {SideNav, SideNavItem, SideNavItemContent, SideNavItemLink, Text} from "@/components/spectrum/SideNav";

<SideNav aria-label="Files" selectedRoute="/guidelines" defaultExpandedKeys={["guidelines"]}>
  <SideNavItem href="/guidelines" id="guidelines" textValue="Guidelines">
    <SideNavItemContent>
      <SideNavItemLink><Text>Guidelines</Text></SideNavItemLink>
    </SideNavItemContent>
  </SideNavItem>
</SideNav>
```

<Canvas of={SideNavStories.Default} />

## Sections

Use `SideNavSection` and `SideNavHeader` to group related items. Sections are non-collapsible and non-interactive.

<Canvas of={SideNavStories.WithSections} />

## Routing and selection

SideNav does not support uncontrolled selection. Manage the current route through `selectedRoute` and update it from your router. Items with an `href` must render a `SideNavItemLink` inside `SideNavItemContent`.

## Collections and nesting

SideNav follows the Collection Components API. Use `items` and a recursive render function for dynamic collections, or use static nested `SideNavItem` children for small trees. Set `defaultExpandedKeys` for initial expansion and `expandedKeys` with `onExpandedChange` for controlled expansion.

## Accessibility

Provide `aria-label` or `aria-labelledby` for the navigation landmark. Give each item a useful `textValue` for typeahead and accessible collection behavior. Use `disabledKeys` for items that cannot be selected or interacted with.

## API

### SideNav

| Name | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `aria-label` | `string` | — | Defines an accessible name for the navigation. |
| `autoFocus` | `boolean \| FocusStrategy` | — | Whether to focus the grid list or an option. |
| `children` | `ReactNode \| (item: T) => ReactNode` | — | The contents of the collection. |
| `defaultExpandedKeys` | `Iterable<Key>` | — | Initial expanded keys in the collection. |
| `disabledKeys` | `Iterable<Key>` | — | Items that cannot be selected or interacted with. |
| `expandedKeys` | `Iterable<Key>` | — | Currently expanded keys in the collection. |
| `id` | `string` | — | The element's unique identifier. |
| `items` | `Iterable<T>` | — | Item objects in a dynamic collection. |
| `onExpandedChange` | `(keys: Set<Key>) => any` | — | Called when items are expanded or collapsed. |
| `selectedRoute` | `string \| null` | — | The route currently selected. |
| `styles` | `StylesPropWithHeight` | — | Spectrum-defined styles. |
| `UNSAFE_className` | `UnsafeClassName` | — | Custom class name, intended only as a last resort. |
| `UNSAFE_style` | `CSSProperties` | — | Inline style, intended only as a last resort. |

### SideNavItem

| Name | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | — | Content and nested children of the item. |
| `hasChildItems` | `boolean` | — | Whether the item has children. |
| `href` | `string` | — | URL to link to. |
| `id` | `Key` | — | Unique item identifier. |
| `isDisabled` | `boolean` | — | Whether the item is disabled. |
| `textValue` | `string` | — | Text representation used for typeahead. |

### SideNavItemContent and SideNavItemLink

Both components accept `children: ReactNode`. `SideNavItemContent` contains the item content and optional actions; `SideNavItemLink` renders the navigable content for items with an `href`.

### SideNavSection and SideNavHeader

`SideNavSection` groups items, while `SideNavHeader` renders the section heading. Sections do not collapse or act as links.

## Controls

<Controls />

TextField is a keyboard text input for custom text entries. It supports labels, descriptio...

<Title />

<Description>
TextField is a keyboard text input for custom text entries. It supports labels, descriptions, validation, prefix decorations, controlled values, and native input types.
</Description>

## Basic usage

<Source language="tsx" code={`import {TextField} from "@/components/spectrum/TextField";

<TextField label="Name" placeholder="Enter your full name" />`} />

<Primary />

## Controlled value

Use `value` and `onChange` when the entered text needs to be stored in application state. Use `defaultValue` for an uncontrolled initial value.

<Source language="tsx" code={`import {useState} from "react";
import {TextField} from "@/components/spectrum/TextField";

function Example() {
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  return <TextField label="Name" value={name} onChange={setName} />;
}`} />

<Stories include={["Controlled", "WithDescription", "Required", "Invalid", "Disabled", "WithPrefix"]} />

## Validation and form integration

Use `isRequired`, `minLength`, `maxLength`, `pattern`, `type`, `validate`, `isInvalid`, and `errorMessage` to communicate validation rules. Provide `name` when submitting the field in a form.

## Accessibility and internationalization

Provide a visible `label` whenever possible. Use `aria-label` only when a visible label cannot be provided. TextField follows the locale and direction from the consuming Spectrum Provider.

## API and Controls

All props are forwarded to the React Spectrum S2 TextField. Important props include `label`, `description`, `placeholder`, `value`, `defaultValue`, `onChange`, `prefix`, `size`, `isDisabled`, `isReadOnly`, `isRequired`, `isInvalid`, `errorMessage`, `type`, and `name`.

<Controls />

## QA color schemes

Use the Storybook Spectrum Light and Spectrum Dark toolbar controls to verify both color schemes.

./src/components/spectrum/Toast/Toast.docs.mdx
# Toast `ToastContainer` renders queued toasts in an application. Place it at the root of ...
import { Meta, Canvas, Controls } from '@storybook/addon-docs/blocks';
import * as Stories from './Toast.stories';

<Meta of={Stories} />

# Toast

`ToastContainer` renders queued toasts in an application. Place it at the root of the application, then use `ToastQueue` to add neutral, positive, negative, or informational messages.

## Basic usage

Render one `Toast` or `ToastContainer` near the application root and enqueue messages from user actions.

<Canvas of={Stories.Default} />

## Variants and actions

Use the variant-specific queue methods for semantic feedback. Pass `actionLabel`, `onAction`, and `shouldCloseOnAction` to add an action button.

<Canvas of={Stories.Variants} />

<Canvas of={Stories.Actionable} />

## Dismissal and accessibility

Use the `timeout` option for automatic dismissal. Toasts have a minimum accessible timeout of five seconds, and actionable toasts do not automatically dismiss. Timers pause when users focus or hover a toast.

The returned close function can dismiss a toast programmatically. Toast content should be concise and meaningful because it is announced through the toast region.

## API

<Controls />

`ToastQueue` exposes `neutral`, `positive`, `negative`, and `info` methods. `ToastContainer` supports `placement` and accessible region props.

    <AccordionItemTitle>Billing Address</AccordionItemTitle>
    <AccordionItemPanel>Billing address form here.</AccordionItemPanel>
  </AccordionItem>
</Accordion>`}
/>

<Primary />

## Expanding

Use `defaultExpandedKeys` for the initial uncontrolled state. Use `expandedKeys` with `onExpandedChange` to control which items are open. The expanded keys correspond to each `AccordionItem` `id`.

<Source
  language="tsx"
  code={`import { useState } from "react";
import {
  Accordion,
  AccordionItem,
  AccordionItemPanel,
  AccordionItemTitle,
  type Key,
} from "@/components/spectrum/Accordion";

function Example() {
const [expandedKeys, setExpandedKeys] = useState(new Set<Key>(["settings"]));

return (
<Accordion expandedKeys={expandedKeys} onExpandedChange={setExpandedKeys}>
<AccordionItem id="settings">
<AccordionItemTitle>Settings</AccordionItemTitle>
<AccordionItemPanel>Application settings content.</AccordionItemPanel>
</AccordionItem>
</Accordion>
);
}`}
/>

Set `allowsMultipleExpanded` when several items may stay open at the same time.

## Content

Use `AccordionItemHeader` to add elements next to an item title, such as icons or action buttons.

<Source
  language="tsx"
  code={`import { ActionButton } from "@react-spectrum/s2/ActionButton";
import {
  Accordion,
  AccordionItem,
  AccordionItemHeader,
  AccordionItemPanel,
  AccordionItemTitle,
} from "@/components/spectrum/Accordion";

<Accordion>
  <AccordionItem id="project">
    <AccordionItemHeader>
      <AccordionItemTitle>Project Settings</AccordionItemTitle>
      <ActionButton aria-label="Edit project settings">Edit</ActionButton>
    </AccordionItemHeader>
    <AccordionItemPanel>
      Configure your project settings including name, description, and permissions.
    </AccordionItemPanel>
  </AccordionItem>
</Accordion>`}
/>

<Stories title="Examples" include={["Controlled", "MultipleExpanded", "WithHeaderActions"]} />

## API

```tsx
<Accordion>
  <AccordionItem>
    <AccordionItemHeader>
      <AccordionItemTitle />
    </AccordionItemHeader>
    <AccordionItemPanel />
  </AccordionItem>
</Accordion>
```

### Accordion

| Name                                | Type                             | Default   | Description                                                        |
| ----------------------------------- | -------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `allowsMultipleExpanded`            | `boolean`                        | —         | Whether multiple accordion items can be expanded at the same time. |
| `defaultExpandedKeys`               | `Iterable<Key>`                  | —         | The initial expanded keys in the uncontrolled state.               |
| `expandedKeys`                      | `Iterable<Key>`                  | —         | The currently expanded keys in the controlled state.               |
| `onExpandedChange`                  | `(keys: Set<Key>) => void`       | —         | Called when accordion items are expanded or collapsed.             |
| `density`                           | `compact \| regular \| spacious` | `regular` | The amount of space between accordion items.                       |
| `size`                              | `S \| M \| L \| XL`              | `M`       | The size of the accordion.                                         |
| `isDisabled`                        | `boolean`                        | `false`   | Whether all accordion items are disabled.                          |
| `isQuiet`                           | `boolean`                        | `false`   | Whether the accordion uses a quiet style.                          |
| `styles`                            | `StylesPropWithHeight`           | —         | Spectrum-defined styles returned by the style macro.               |
| `UNSAFE_className` / `UNSAFE_style` | `string` / `CSSProperties`       | —         | Last-resort custom CSS escape hatches.                             |

### AccordionItem

| Name                         | Type                            | Default   | Description                                       |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------------------- |
| `id`                         | `Key`                           | —         | Identifier that matches `expandedKeys`.           |
| `defaultExpanded`            | `boolean`                       | `false`   | Whether the item is initially expanded.           |
| `isExpanded`                 | `boolean`                       | —         | Controlled expanded state for an individual item. |
| `onExpandedChange`           | `(isExpanded: boolean) => void` | —         | Called when the item changes expanded state.      |
| `isDisabled`                 | `boolean`                       | `false`   | Whether the item is disabled.                     |
| `density`, `size`, `isQuiet` | —                               | Inherited | Item-level display options.                       |

### AccordionItemHeader and AccordionItemTitle

`AccordionItemHeader` accepts the header content. `AccordionItemTitle` renders the item heading and accepts `level`, which defaults to `3`. Both also support `id`, `UNSAFE_className`, and `UNSAFE_style`.

### AccordionItemPanel

`AccordionItemPanel` contains the expandable content. It supports standard accessibility labels and `role`, which defaults to `group`; use `region` when the panel needs a landmark role.

## Controls

Use the Storybook Controls panel to QA `allowsMultipleExpanded`, `density`, `isDisabled`, `isQuiet`, and `size`.

<Controls />

./src/components/spectrum/ActionBar/ActionBar.docs.mdx
Action bars are used for single and bulk selection patterns when a user needs to perform a...
import { Meta, Title, Description, Primary, Controls, Source } from "@storybook/addon-docs/blocks";
import * as ActionBarStories from "./ActionBar.stories";

<Meta of={ActionBarStories} />
<Title />
<Description>
Action bars are used for single and bulk selection patterns when a user needs to perform actions on one or more items at the same time. This wrapper automatically applies React Spectrum S2 styles, typography, color schemes, and accessibility behavior.
</Description>

## QA color scheme

Use the **Spectrum Light** or **Spectrum Dark** toolbar control in Storybook to inspect both color schemes.

## Basic usage

Pass `selectedItemCount` and place one or more `ActionButton` components inside the bar. The bar is hidden when the count is `0`.

<Source language="tsx" code={`import { ActionBar, ActionButton } from "@/components/spectrum/ActionBar";

<ActionBar selectedItemCount={2}>
  <ActionButton aria-label="Edit">Edit</ActionButton>
  <ActionButton aria-label="Copy">Copy</ActionButton>
  <ActionButton aria-label="Delete">Delete</ActionButton>
</ActionBar>`} />
<Primary />

## TableView, ListView, and TreeView

ActionBar is intended to be rendered by collection components through `renderActionBar`. Pass the selected collection size to `selectedItemCount` and keep the actions inside the bar. The same pattern works with `ListView` and `TreeView`.

<Source language="tsx" code={`<TableView
  aria-label="Table with action bar"
  selectionMode="multiple"
  renderActionBar={(selectedKeys) => (
    <ActionBar selectedItemCount={selectedKeys.size}>
      <ActionButton aria-label="Edit">Edit</ActionButton>
      <ActionButton aria-label="Copy">Copy</ActionButton>
      <ActionButton aria-label="Delete">Delete</ActionButton>
    </ActionBar>
  )}
>`} />

## Clearing selection

Use `onClearSelection` to connect the built-in clear-selection button to the state owned by the collection.

<Source language="tsx" code={`<ActionBar
  selectedItemCount={selectedKeys.size}
  onClearSelection={() => setSelectedKeys(new Set())}
>
  <ActionButton aria-label="Archive">Archive</ActionButton>
</ActionBar>`} />

## All items and emphasis

Use `selectedItemCount="all"` when every item is selected. Set `isEmphasized` to `true` when the actions need stronger visual emphasis.

<Source language="tsx" code={`<ActionBar selectedItemCount="all" isEmphasized>
  <ActionButton aria-label="Archive">Archive</ActionButton>
</ActionBar>`} />

## API

  <thead>
    <tr><th>Name</th><th>Type</th><th>Default</th><th>Description</th></tr>

  <tbody>
    <tr><td><code>children</code></td><td><code>ReactNode</code></td><td>-</td><td>A list of ActionButtons to display.</td></tr>
    <tr><td><code>id</code></td><td><code>string | undefined</code></td><td>-</td><td>The element's unique identifier.</td></tr>
    <tr><td><code>isEmphasized</code></td><td><code>boolean | undefined</code></td><td>-</td><td>Whether the ActionBar uses an emphasized style.</td></tr>
    <tr><td><code>onClearSelection</code></td><td><code>(() =&gt; void) | undefined</code></td><td>-</td><td>Handler called when the clear-selection button is pressed.</td></tr>
    <tr><td><code>scrollRef</code></td><td><code>RefObject&lt;HTMLElement | null&gt; | undefined</code></td><td>-</td><td>Ref to the scrollable element above which the bar appears.</td></tr>
    <tr><td><code>selectedItemCount</code></td><td><code>number | "all" | undefined</code></td><td>-</td><td>Number of selected items; hidden when <code>0</code>.</td></tr>
    <tr><td><code>slot</code></td><td><code>string | null | undefined</code></td><td>-</td><td>Slot name for receiving parent props.</td></tr>
    <tr><td><code>styles</code></td><td><code>StylesProp | undefined</code></td><td>-</td><td>Spectrum styles returned by the <code>style()</code> macro.</td></tr>

    <tr><td><code>UNSAFE_style</code></td><td><code>CSSProperties | undefined</code></td><td>-</td><td>Inline style, intended only as a last resort.</td></tr>
  </tbody>
</table>

`ActionButton` is re-exported from this wrapper for convenience. Its props include `aria-label`, `isDisabled`, `isQuiet`, `isPending`, `onPress`, and other React Spectrum S2 action behavior.

<Title />
<Description>
ActionButtons allow users to perform an action. They are used for similar, task-based options within a workflow and are ideal for interfaces where buttons are not meant to draw a lot of attention. This wrapper automatically applies React Spectrum S2 styling, typography, color schemes, and accessibility behavior.
</Description>

## QA color scheme

Use the **Spectrum Light** or **Spectrum Dark** toolbar control in Storybook to inspect every state in both color schemes.

## Basic usage

<Source language="tsx" code={`import { ActionButton } from "@/components/spectrum/ActionButton";

<ActionButton>Edit</ActionButton>`} />
<Primary />

ActionButtons can contain an icon, text, avatar, or other Spectrum content. Provide `aria-label` when the button has no visible text label.

<Source language="tsx" code={`import { ActionButton } from "@/components/spectrum/ActionButton";
import Cut from "@react-spectrum/s2/icons/Cut";

<ActionButton aria-label="Cut"><Cut />Cut</ActionButton>`} />

## Events

Use `onPress` to handle mouse, keyboard, and touch interactions. `onPressStart`, `onPressEnd`, and `onPressChange` are also emitted during the interaction lifecycle.

<Source language="tsx" code={`import { useState } from "react";
import { ActionButton } from "@/components/spectrum/ActionButton";

function Example() {
  const [count, setCount] = useState(0);

  return (
    <ActionButton onPress={() => setCount((value) => value + 1)}>
      {count} Edits
    </ActionButton>
  );
}`} />

## Pending

Set `isPending` to display an indeterminate spinner. Pending buttons remain focusable but do not accept press or hover interactions while pending.

<Source language="tsx" code={`import { useState } from "react";
import { ActionButton } from "@/components/spectrum/ActionButton";

function PendingButton() {
  const [isPending, setPending] = useState(false);

  return (
    <ActionButton
      isPending={isPending}
      onPress={() => {
        setPending(true);
        setTimeout(() => setPending(false), 5000);
      }}>
      Save
    </ActionButton>
  );
}`} />

## Appearance and state

- `isQuiet` applies the quiet ActionButton style.
- `isDisabled` disables the button.
- `size` supports `XS`, `S`, `M`, `L`, and `XL`.
- `staticColor` supports `auto`, `black`, and `white` when the button appears over a colored background.
- Use `type="submit"` or `type="reset"` when the button participates in a form.

<Stories title="Examples" include={["WithIcon", "Quiet", "Disabled", "Pending", "InteractivePending"]} />

<table>
  <thead><tr><th>Name</th><th>Type</th><th>Default</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead>

An ActionButtonGroup is a grouping of related ActionButtons. This wrapper uses React Spect...

<Title />
<Description>
An ActionButtonGroup is a grouping of related ActionButtons. This wrapper uses React Spectrum S2 and automatically applies Spectrum styling, typography, color schemes, and accessibility behavior.
</Description>

## QA color scheme

Use the **Spectrum Light** or **Spectrum Dark** toolbar control in Storybook to inspect the group in both color schemes.

## Basic usage

Place related ActionButtons inside the group. Use `Text` alongside an icon to provide visible labels.

<Source language="tsx" code={`import { ActionButtonGroup, ActionButton, Text } from "@/components/spectrum/ActionButtonGroup";
import Cut from "@react-spectrum/s2/icons/Cut";
import Copy from "@react-spectrum/s2/icons/Copy";
import Paste from "@react-spectrum/s2/icons/Paste";

<ActionButtonGroup>
  <ActionButton aria-label="Cut"><Cut /><Text>Cut</Text></ActionButton>
  <ActionButton aria-label="Copy"><Copy /><Text>Copy</Text></ActionButton>
  <ActionButton aria-label="Paste"><Paste /><Text>Paste</Text></ActionButton>
</ActionButtonGroup>`} />
<Primary />

## Orientation and density

Set `orientation="vertical"` to stack buttons. Use `density="compact"` for tighter spacing, or keep the default `regular` density for standard spacing.

<Source language="tsx" code={`<ActionButtonGroup orientation="vertical" density="compact">
  <ActionButton aria-label="Cut">Cut</ActionButton>
  <ActionButton aria-label="Copy">Copy</ActionButton>
</ActionButtonGroup>`} />

## Justified and quiet groups

Set `isJustified` to divide the available width equally between buttons. Set `isQuiet` when the group should use the quiet ActionButton style. Use `staticColor="black"` or `staticColor="white"` when the group appears over a colored background.

## Disabled state

Set `isDisabled` on the group to disable all ActionButtons in the group consistently.

<Stories title="Examples" include={["Vertical", "Compact", "Justified", "Disabled", "Quiet"]} />

## API

<table>
  <thead><tr><th>Name</th><th>Type</th><th>Default</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead>
  <tbody>
    <tr><td><code>aria-describedby</code></td><td><code>string | undefined</code></td><td>-</td><td>Identifies the element or elements that describe the group.</td></tr>
    <tr><td><code>aria-details</code></td><td><code>string | undefined</code></td><td>-</td><td>Identifies detailed information about the group.</td></tr>
    <tr><td><code>aria-label</code></td><td><code>string | undefined</code></td><td>-</td><td>Defines an accessible label for the group.</td></tr>
    <tr><td><code>aria-labelledby</code></td><td><code>string | undefined</code></td><td>-</td><td>References the element that labels the group.</td></tr>
    <tr><td><code>children</code></td><td><code>ReactNode</code></td><td>-</td><td>The ActionButton children of the group.</td></tr>
    <tr><td><code>density</code></td><td><code>"compact" | "regular" | undefined</code></td><td><code>regular</code></td><td>Spacing between the buttons.</td></tr>
    <tr><td><code>isDisabled</code></td><td><code>boolean | undefined</code></td><td>-</td><td>Whether the group is disabled.</td></tr>

./src/components/spectrum/ActionMenu/ActionMenu.docs.mdx
# ActionMenu ActionMenu combines an ActionButton with a Menu for simple “more actions” use...
import {Canvas, Controls, Meta} from '@storybook/addon-docs/blocks';
import * as ActionMenuStories from './ActionMenu.stories';

<Meta of={ActionMenuStories} />

# ActionMenu

ActionMenu combines an ActionButton with a Menu for simple “more actions” use cases. It provides Spectrum styling, keyboard navigation, focus management, and accessible menu semantics.

## Basic usage

```tsx
import {ActionMenu, Keyboard, MenuItem, Text} from '@/components/spectrum/ActionMenu';

<ActionMenu aria-label="More actions">
  <MenuItem textValue="Copy" onAction={() => copySelection()}>
    <Text slot="label">Copy</Text>
    <Text slot="description">Copy the selected text</Text>
    <Keyboard>⌘C</Keyboard>
  </MenuItem>
</ActionMenu>
```

<Canvas of={ActionMenuStories.Default} />

## Variants and states

Use `isQuiet` for a low-emphasis trigger, `isDisabled` to disable the entire menu, and `disabledKeys` to disable specific menu items. `size` controls the trigger while `menuSize` controls the menu.

<Canvas of={ActionMenuStories.Quiet} />
<Canvas of={ActionMenuStories.DisabledItems} />

## Controlled open state

Use `isOpen` with `onOpenChange` when the open state must be controlled by the parent.

<Canvas of={ActionMenuStories.Controlled} />

## Placement and accessibility

Use `direction`, `align`, and `shouldFlip` to control placement. The menu flips automatically by default when space is limited. Provide an accessible name with `aria-label` or `aria-labelledby`, and use `textValue` for items whose visible content is not plain text.

## API

| Name | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `align` | `"end" \| "start"` | `start` | Alignment of the menu relative to the trigger. |
| `children` | `ReactNode \| (item: T) => ReactNode` | — | The contents of the collection. |
| `defaultOpen` | `boolean` | — | Whether the overlay is open by default. |
| `direction` | `"bottom" \| "end" \| "left" \| "right" \| "start" \| "top"` | `bottom` | Where the menu opens relative to its trigger. |
| `disabledKeys` | `Iterable<Key>` | — | Items that cannot be selected or focused. |
| `isDisabled` | `boolean` | — | Whether the button is disabled. |
| `isOpen` | `boolean` | — | Whether the overlay is controlled as open. |
| `isQuiet` | `boolean` | — | Whether the trigger uses a quiet style. |
| `items` | `Iterable<T>` | — | Item objects in the collection. |
| `menuSize` | `"L" \| "M" \| "S" \| "XL"` | `M` | The size of the Menu. |
| `onAction` | `(key: Key, value: T) => void` | — | Called when an item is selected. |
| `onOpenChange` | `(isOpen: boolean) => void` | — | Called when the overlay open state changes. |
| `shouldCloseOnSelect` | `boolean` | — | Whether the menu closes when an item is selected. |
| `shouldFlip` | `boolean` | `true` | Whether the menu automatically flips when space is limited. |
| `size` | `"L" \| "M" \| "S" \| "XL" \| "XS"` | `M` | Size of the ActionButton trigger. |
| `styles` | `StylesProp` | — | Spectrum-defined styles. |
| `UNSAFE_className` | `UnsafeClassName` | — | Custom class name, intended only as a last resort. |
| `UNSAFE_style` | `CSSProperties` | — | Inline style, intended only as a last resort. |

## Controls

# Avatar An avatar is a thumbnail representation of an entity, such as a user or an organi...
import {Canvas, Controls, Meta} from "@storybook/addon-docs/blocks";

# Avatar

An avatar is a thumbnail representation of an entity, such as a user or an organization. This wrapper uses React Spectrum S2 styling and preserves its accessible image behavior.

## Basic usage

```tsx
import {Avatar} from "@/components/spectrum/Avatar";

<Avatar src="/images/user.png" alt="Alex Johnson" />
```

<Canvas of={AvatarStories.Default} />

## Sizes

Use the `size` prop to select a supported Spectrum size or provide a numeric size. The default size is `24`.

<Canvas of={AvatarStories.Small} />
<Canvas of={AvatarStories.Large} />

## Backgrounds and fallback

Set `isOverBackground` when the avatar appears over a color background. If no `src` is supplied, Spectrum renders the appropriate fallback treatment.

<Canvas of={AvatarStories.OverBackground} />
<Canvas of={AvatarStories.WithoutImage} />

## Accessibility

Always provide meaningful `alt` text when the avatar communicates identity. If the avatar is purely decorative, use an empty `alt` value. The image URL is passed through the `src` prop.

## API

| Name | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `alt` | `string` | — | Text description of the avatar. |
| `id` | `string` | — | The element's unique identifier. |
| `isOverBackground` | `boolean` | — | Whether the avatar is over a color background. |
| `size` | `16 \| 20 \| 24 \| 28 \| 32 \| 36 \| 40 \| 44 \| 48 \| 56 \| 64 \| 80 \| 96 \| 112 \| number` | `24` | The size of the avatar. |
| `slot` | `string \| null` | — | A slot name for receiving parent props. |
| `src` | `string` | — | The image URL for the avatar. |
| `styles` | `StylesPropWithoutWidth` | — | Spectrum-defined styles returned by the style macro. |
| `UNSAFE_className` | `UnsafeClassName` | — | Custom class name, intended only as a last resort. |
| `UNSAFE_style` | `CSSProperties` | — | Inline style, intended only as a last resort. |

| `children` | `ReactNode` | — | Avatar children of the avatar group. |

| `size` | `16 \| 20 \| 24 \| 28 \| 32 \| 36 \| 40` | `24` | The size of the avatar group. |
| `slot` | `string \| null` | — | A slot name for receiving parent props. |
| `styles` | `StylesPropWithoutWidth` | — | Spectrum-defined styles returned by the style macro. |

# Badge

Badges show a small amount of color-categorized metadata and are ideal for getting a user's attention.

## Basic usage

```tsx
import {Badge} from "@/components/spectrum/Badge";

<Badge variant="informative">In progress</Badge>
```

<Canvas of={BadgeStories.Default} />

## Semantic variants

Use semantic variants such as `positive`, `negative`, `notice`, or `informative` when the badge communicates meaning. Use color variants such as `blue`, `purple`, or `green` for non-semantic categorization.

<Canvas of={BadgeStories.SemanticPositive} />
<Canvas of={BadgeStories.SemanticNegative} />

## Fill styles and overflow

Use `bold`, `outline`, or `subtle` with `fillStyle` to control the visual treatment. Set `overflowMode` to `truncate` when the badge must stay on one line, or use `wrap` for longer content.

<Canvas of={BadgeStories.Outline} />
<Canvas of={BadgeStories.Subtle} />
<Canvas of={BadgeStories.Truncated} />

## Accessibility

Badge content should be concise and understandable without relying only on color. Use semantic variants when applicable and provide `aria-label` when the visible content does not fully describe the badge.

## API

| Name | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `aria-describedby` | `string` | — | Identifies the element that describes the badge. |
| `aria-details` | `string` | — | Identifies detailed information about the badge. |
| `aria-label` | `string` | — | Defines an accessible name for the badge. |
| `aria-labelledby` | `string` | — | References the element that labels the badge. |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | — | The content to display in the badge. |
| `fillStyle` | `"bold" \| "outline" \| "subtle"` | `bold` | The fill treatment of the badge. |
| `id` | `string` | — | The element's unique identifier. |
| `overflowMode` | `"truncate" \| "wrap"` | `wrap` | Sets text behavior for badge contents. |
| `size` | `"S" \| "M" \| "L" \| "XL"` | `S` | The size of the badge. |
| `slot` | `string \| null` | — | A slot name for receiving parent props. |
| `styles` | `StylesProp` | — | Spectrum-defined styles returned by the style macro. |
| `UNSAFE_className` | `UnsafeClassName` | — | Custom class name, intended only as a last resort. |
| `UNSAFE_style` | `CSSProperties` | — | Inline style, intended only as a last resort. |
| `variant` | `accent \| blue \| brown \| celery \| chartreuse \| cinnamon \| cyan \| fuchsia \| gray \| green \| indigo \| informative \| magenta \| negative \| neutral \| notice \| orange \| pink \| positive \| purple \| red \| seafoam \| silver \| turquoise \| yellow` | `neutral` | Changes the background color or semantic meaning of the badge. |

# Button Buttons allow users to perform an action. This wrapper exposes the React Spectrum...
import { Meta, Canvas, Controls } from '@storybook/addon-docs/blocks';
import * as Stories from './Button.stories';

<Meta of={Stories} />

# Button

Buttons allow users to perform an action. This wrapper exposes the React Spectrum S2 Button API while applying the local Spectrum baseline automatically.

## Basic usage

Use the `Button` component with an `onPress` handler. The `onPress` event works consistently with mouse, keyboard, and touch interactions.

<Canvas of={Stories.Primary} />

## Variants and sizes

Use `variant` to communicate the action hierarchy and `size` to match the surrounding layout. `fillStyle="outline"` creates an outlined button.

<Canvas of={Stories.VariantsAndSizes} />

## Disabled and pending states

Use `isDisabled` when the action cannot currently be performed. Use `isPending` for asynchronous work; pending buttons remain focusable while their interaction is disabled and Spectrum announces the pending state.

<Canvas of={Stories.Disabled} />

<Canvas of={Stories.Pending} />

## Events

Use `onPress`, `onPressStart`, `onPressEnd`, or `onPressChange` for interaction callbacks. These callbacks are local Storybook fixtures and do not call an external service.

<Canvas of={Stories.PressEvents} />

## Accessibility and theming

Use visible text for the action whenever possible. For icon-only buttons, provide an accessible `aria-label`. Button styles, color scheme, focus behavior, and keyboard interaction are provided by React Spectrum S2. Verify the component in both Spectrum Light and Dark themes.

## API

<Controls />

The wrapper supports the full S2 Button API, including `variant`, `fillStyle`, `size`, `isDisabled`, `isPending`, `type`, `onPress`, `styles`, and accessibility properties.

<Title />

<Description>
Calendar menampilkan grid hari dalam satu atau beberapa bulan untuk memilih satu tanggal. Component ini adalah wrapper React Spectrum S2, sehingga style dan aksesibilitas Spectrum aktif otomatis saat diimpor dari `@/components/spectrum/Calendar`.
</Description>

## QA color scheme

Gunakan toolbar **Spectrum Light** atau **Spectrum Dark** di bagian atas Storybook untuk memeriksa tampilan Calendar pada kedua color scheme. Kontrol ini hanya memengaruhi story kategori Spectrum.

## Penggunaan dasar

<Source
  language="tsx"
  code={`import { Calendar } from "@/components/spectrum/Calendar";

<Calendar aria-label="Tanggal pilihan" />`}
/>

`aria-label` wajib diberikan apabila Calendar tidak memiliki label visual yang terhubung. Gunakan `defaultValue` untuk nilai awal yang tidak dikontrol.

<Primary />

## Nilai controlled

Gunakan `value` dan `onChange` ketika tanggal perlu disimpan pada state atau disinkronkan dengan form.

<Source
  language="tsx"
  code={`import { parseDate } from "@internationalized/date";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Calendar } from "@/components/spectrum/Calendar";

function Example() {
  const [date, setDate] = useState(parseDate("2025-02-03"));

  return <Calendar aria-label="Tanggal pilihan" value={date} onChange={setDate} />;
}`}
/>

<Stories title="Contoh interaktif" include={["Controlled", "TwoMonths", "Disabled"]} />

## Validasi dan ketersediaan tanggal

Batasi rentang dengan `minValue` dan `maxValue`. Gunakan `isDateUnavailable` untuk aturan tambahan seperti tanggal penuh atau akhir pekan. `isInvalid` dan `errorMessage` digunakan jika aturan validasi berasal dari aplikasi.

<Source
  language="tsx"
  code={`import { getLocalTimeZone, isWeekend, today } from "@internationalized/date";

const now = today(getLocalTimeZone());

<Calendar
  aria-label="Tanggal janji temu"
  minValue={now}
  maxValue={now.add({ months: 3 })}
  isDateUnavailable={(date) => isWeekend(date, "id-ID")}
/>`}
/>

## Tampilan dan fokus

- `visibleMonths` menampilkan beberapa bulan sekaligus.
- `firstDayOfWeek` mengatur awal minggu.
- `focusedValue` dan `onFocusChange` mengontrol bulan yang sedang terlihat.
- `pageBehavior="visible"` berpindah sebesar jumlah bulan terlihat; gunakan `"single"` untuk berpindah satu bulan.
- `isDisabled` menonaktifkan Calendar; `isReadOnly` mempertahankan nilai tetapi tidak mengizinkan perubahan.

## Internasionalisasi

Calendar memakai locale pengguna secara default. Berikan nilai dari `@internationalized/date` untuk parsing, manipulasi tanggal, zona waktu, atau sistem kalender lain. Untuk mengubah sistem kalender tampilan, gunakan `Provider` Spectrum dengan locale yang memakai Unicode calendar extension.

<Source
  language="tsx"
  code={`import { parseDate } from "@internationalized/date";
import { Provider } from "@react-spectrum/s2/Provider";
import { Calendar } from "@/components/spectrum/Calendar";

<Provider locale="id-ID-u-ca-gregory">
  <Calendar aria-label="Tanggal pilihan" defaultValue={parseDate("2025-02-03")} />
</Provider>`}
/>

## API dan Controls

Gunakan panel Controls untuk mencoba props yang dapat dikonfigurasi. Props penting lainnya yang diteruskan langsung ke Calendar Spectrum: `defaultValue`, `value`, `onChange`, `minValue`, `maxValue`, `isDateUnavailable`, `focusedValue`, `onFocusChange`, `selectionAlignment`, `errorMessage`, dan `createCalendar`.

<Controls />

## Basic usage

<Source language="tsx" code={`import {Card, CardPreview, Content, Image, Text} from "@/components/spectrum/Card";

<Card aria-label="Project Aurora">
  <CardPreview><Image alt="Project preview" src={preview} /></CardPreview>
  <Content>
    <Text slot="title">Project Aurora</Text>
    <Text slot="description">A concise project overview.</Text>
  </Content>
</Card>`} />

<Primary />

## Variants and states

Use `size`, `density`, and `variant` to adjust the card presentation. Use `isDisabled` to prevent interaction and `href` to make the card navigable. The `AssetCard`, `UserCard`, and `ProductCard` variants provide predefined layouts.

<Controls />
<Stories include={["Quiet", "Disabled", "Linked", "Asset", "User", "Product"]} />

## Accessibility and internationalization

Provide an accessible label when the card does not contain an equivalent visible label. Card content uses the Spectrum S2 locale and color scheme context supplied by the consuming application boundary.

## QA color schemes

Use the Storybook Spectrum Light and Spectrum Dark toolbar controls to verify both color schemes.

# ComboBox ComboBox allows users to choose a single option from a collapsible list when sp...
import { Meta, Canvas, Controls } from '@storybook/addon-docs/blocks';
import * as Stories from './ComboBox.stories';

<Meta of={Stories} />

# ComboBox

ComboBox allows users to choose a single option from a collapsible list when space is limited.

## Basic usage

Use `ComboBoxItem` children with stable `id` values for selectable options. The wrapper preserves the React Spectrum S2 API and automatically applies the Spectrum baseline through `.spectrum-component`.

<Canvas of={Stories.Default} />

## Content and sections

Items can contain label and description slots. Use `ComboBoxSection` with `Header`, `Heading`, and `Text` to group related options.

<Canvas of={Stories.WithDescriptions} />

<Canvas of={Stories.WithSections} />

## State

Use `defaultSelectedKey` for uncontrolled selection or `selectedKey` with `onSelectionChange` for controlled selection. `isDisabled`, `isRequired`, `isInvalid`, `loadingState`, and `onLoadMore` support common form and asynchronous states.

<Canvas of={Stories.Disabled} />

## Accessibility

Provide a visible `label` or an accessible `aria-label`. ComboBox items should contain text or decorative graphics only; interactive controls inside items break keyboard and screen reader navigation.

## API

<Controls />

See the React Spectrum S2 ComboBox API for the complete prop and collection contract.

import { Meta, Canvas, Controls } from '@storybook/addon-docs/blocks';

<Meta of={Stories} />

# InlineAlert

`InlineAlert` displays non-modal feedback associated with content in a view, such as form validation or operation status.

## Basic usage

Use `Heading` and `Content` from this wrapper to provide an accessible alert structure.

<Canvas of={Stories.Default} />

## Variants

Use semantic variants such as `negative`, `positive`, `notice`, `informative`, and `neutral` with `border`, `subtleFill`, or `boldFill` fill styles.

<Canvas of={Stories.Negative} />

<Controls />

import { forwardRef } from "react";
import { LabeledValue as SpectrumLabeledValue, type LabeledValueProps } from "@react-spectrum/s2/LabeledValue";

# LinkButton

This compatibility adapter combines the Spectrum Button appearance with link navigation. The installed S2 package does not currently export a `LinkButton` subpath, so navigation is handled by the adapter while visual behavior comes from the official S2 Button.

<Canvas of={Stories.Basic} />

Use `href`, `target`, `rel`, `isDisabled`, and the regular S2 button props. Browser navigation is not triggered while disabled.
